### 7.2 Blinds and curtains — per room

**Plain curtains on ceiling tracks, everywhere.** A curtain on a track has no cord loop, so it meets the child-safety test without a motor. CPSC and EN 13120 both turn on whether a child can reach a loop, and chain roller blinds keep one. The flat doesn't need rollers anyway: the Living has no outside window and the Drawing faces north.

#### Window inventory

Read from the brochure plan (orientation confirmed 2026-09-20: dining sit-out east, Master balcony south) and §7.4's trace. **Confirm each opening, its width and its height on site.** Floor 42 has no shading from other towers, so any east or south glass takes full sun.

| Room | Opening and facing | Sun load | Covering | Motor? |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Drawing | One window, north | Low | Sheer + light-filtering lined drape | No |
| Living | None to outside | None | Nothing | No |
| Dining | Sliding door to sit-out, east | High, 7–10 AM, Mar–Jun | Sheer + blackout-lined drape. Optional film | Optional, one Aqara E1 |
| Kitchen, utility | South window, east utility | Medium | Nothing, or a cordless roller if glare bothers you | No |
| Master | Sliding door to balcony, south. §7.4 also reads a small west window: check on site | Medium, high-angle | Sheer + blackout drape, track to the ceiling | Optional, one Aqara E1 |
| BR-01 | Window, south | Medium | Sheer for calls + blackout drape for guests | No |
| BR-02 Kabir | Window, north | Low | Blackout drape for naps + sheer | No |
| BR-03 Gurudwara | Window, north half of the east wall (§7.4). The brochure may show a north window too | **High, 7–10 AM** | Fine sheer + light-filtering lined drape on its own track, drawn in the mornings. Optional film | No |

That's 6–7 curtain runs.

**BR-03 sun and the Palki.** §7.4 puts the Palki on the north wall and the window in the north half of the east wall. So the low morning sun crosses the north half of the room, where the Palki stands. The old note wanted "soft, indirect daylight, never harsh direct sun" on the Saroop. Close the lined drape from sunrise to about 10 AM in Mar–Jun, or film the glass. Before Homz draws, stand in the room at 8 AM and check where the beam lands.

#### Rules

1. **Track, not rod.** A ceiling-fixed double track (sheer behind, drape in front) hides the head and runs the curtain wall to wall. Homz's false-ceiling team can recess it; ask for a curtain pocket in the ceiling drawing.
2. **Wand, not cord.** Local tracks can come cord-drawn. Specify wand-drawn or hand-drawn, with no pull cord, so there's no loop.
3. **Measure the drop first.** IKEA India's ready-made curtains are 250 cm long and can only be shortened (HÄLLEBRÄCKA, MAJGULL and BENGTA all come at 250 cm). A ceiling track at ~2.7–2.9 m (ceiling height **unverified**) needs ~265–285 cm, so plan on made-to-measure. Use IKEA only where the track sits at ≤2.55 m.
4. **Blackout lining where people sleep or nap:** Master, BR-01, BR-02. Write "blackout lining" on the order. "Room-darkening" fabric is weaker: IKEA says MAJGULL room-darkening "lets in a small amount of light". The drape should overlap the wall by 15 cm each side.
5. **One motor at most, battery-powered.** The Aqara Curtain Driver E1 hangs on the track and runs on a 6,000 mAh battery, so there's no wiring. It needs an Aqara Zigbee 3.0 hub; §1 lists Hub M2 at ₹7,400. One driver pulls a one-way curtain. A centre-split pair needs two, grouped in the app. Track Version ₹15,220, Rod Version ₹17,610, 12 kg load limit (Modo India). The Track Version fits U-rails and I-rails only. Tell Homz to fit a U- or I-rail, or buy the Rod Version.
6. **Sun and fade.** East and south glass fade fabric, wood and art. Line every drape facing east or south. Keep originals off those walls (§10).

#### Pricing by width

**Made-to-measure is the default.** The figures below are estimates, not quotes (**unverified**). Before relying on them, get Homz's BOQ curtain line and one quote from a Hyderabad curtain shop.

Track length is the opening width plus ~30 cm for the stack. A good fullness is about twice the track length.

| Track length | Made-to-measure, sheer + lined drape + track (estimate, **unverified**) | IKEA build, only if the track sits at ≤2.55 m |
|---|---|---|
| Up to 140 cm | ~₹8,000–12,000 | 1 VIDGA 2-track set (₹2,450) + 1 HÄLLEBRÄCKA pair (₹1,990) + 1 BENGTA block-out (₹999) = ₹5,439 |
| 141–280 cm | ~₹12,000–20,000 | Double the above = ₹10,878 |
| 281–420 cm | ~₹18,000–30,000 | Triple = ₹16,317 |

Widths read off the plan (±30 cm, **unverified**): Drawing ~180 cm, Dining slider ~240 cm, Master slider 150–300 cm (§7.4 reads 4'11"), BR-01 ~120 cm, BR-02 ~150 cm, BR-03 ~180 cm. That gives a made-to-measure total of about **₹1.3–2L** for 6–7 runs (estimate). An IKEA total isn't worth stating until the track height is measured, because 250 cm drops probably won't reach.

IKEA notes, if a track does sit low enough: the VIDGA 2-track set has two 140 cm rails. IKEA's page describes joining track segments with the supplied connectors. BENGTA is a true block-out in beige, 210×250 cm per piece. MAJGULL comes as "block-out" (dark green) or "room-darkening" (light grey); the room-darkening one isn't enough for the bedrooms.

#### Per-room spec

| Room | Track | Sheer | Drape |
|---|---|---|---|
| Drawing | Ceiling double | Linen-look, putty | Light-filtering, lined, putty |
| Dining | Ceiling double | Linen-look | Blackout-lined |
| Master | Ceiling double, U- or I-rail if the E1 is fitted | Linen-look | Blackout-lined, one-way stack for the E1 |
| BR-01 | Ceiling double | Linen-look | Blackout-lined |
| BR-02 | Ceiling double | Cotton | Blackout-lined, muted sage or ochre |
| BR-03 | Ceiling double | Fine white or beige sheer | Light-filtering, lined, white or ivory |

#### Window film (optional)

Film is the only fix that works while the curtains are open. Clear spectrally selective film cuts UV and heat with little visible tint. Apply it to the Dining east slider and the BR-03 east window, about 90 sqft in total (estimate).

| Item | Detail |
|---|---|
| Product class | Clear spectrally selective, e.g. 3M Prestige 70. Old note also named Garware; LLumar is an Eastman brand, not Garware |
| Price | ₹550–600/sqft on IndiaMART listings for 3M Prestige 70 (2026-10-01). Listings often exclude GST. About ₹50–55k for 90 sqft (**unverified**) |
| Before ordering | Get the RWA's façade rule, and the UPVC or glass maker's position on film over its glass. Room-side film absorbs heat, which can crack large panes or break a double-glazed unit's seal and void its warranty (old note) |
| Who fits it | The film brand's authorised installer. Not Homz, unless Homz subcontracts one |

Skip film if you'll draw the lined drapes in the morning. That costs nothing and does most of the job for the Saroop and the dining table.

- **Tuya/Dooya tubular roller motors.** Their whole case was child-safe rollers. Curtains already are.
- **230 V point inside every pelmet.** New electrical work. The battery E1 removes it.
- **3% solar-screen and honeycomb blinds.** They were for an east-facing Drawing and a sunny Living. Per the plan, neither exists.

#### How the plans were drawn

- Traced from the brochure plan (p.32) at ~28.5 px per foot, then redrawn at 1 ft = 24 px. Room sizes come from the brochure labels. Door, window and opening positions are read off the drawing and may be out by ±6 in.
- Furniture uses the seller's published size. Where a size isn't published, the plan says "~".
- Clearance targets: 900 mm for any walk, 400–450 mm between a sofa and its table. For dining, table edge to wall ≥1,100 mm, so someone can pass a seated guest. A seated diner pushes the chair back ~300 mm, so a tucked-chair figure overstates the space. These are common planning rules of thumb, not a standard (unverified).
- Every door is drawn with its swing, and so are the shoe cabinet's six 305 mm shutters. Bedroom doors swing into the bedroom, so none of them blocks Living furniture. The closet and store doors swing out into the Living, per the private-rooms plan.

#### Drawing room + foyer (13'0" × 17'8", N window)

<img src="../../../assets/personal/olympus/render-drawing.jpg" width="480"> <img src="../../../assets/personal/olympus/room-drawing.svg" width="320">

*The render (2026-10-02) composites the actual product photos into a perspective drawn from these room dimensions, at seller sizes. The plan is redrawn for the 244 cm Porto and 66.7 cm Rhodes.*

**Layout.** Main door at the NW corner, hinged on the north side. A 1830 × 350 mm full-height shoe cabinet and a 900 × 400 mm bench run along the west wall. One small artwork hangs above the bench, lit by one spot. The 244 cm Porto sofa backs onto the solid south wall, centred, with the art above it and two ceiling spots aimed at it. Two 66.7 cm Rhodes armchairs face the sofa across the oval table. The north half stays open as the entry landing. The Era 300 stands on a plinth in the SW corner and the arc lamp's base tucks under the sofa's west end. Hang every frame on security hangers, because a 6-year-old will stand on the sofa and the bench.

| Clearance | Measured on the plan | Target |
|---|---|---|
| Walk from door to bench, between the shoe cabinet and chair 1 | 900 mm (600 with one shutter open) | ≥900 |
| Walk from door to Living, east side | 978 mm | ≥900 |
| Between the two armchairs | 393 mm | ≥300 |
| Sofa ends to side walls | 761 mm each | n/a (west: plinth and lamp; east: beside the Living opening) |
| Sofa front to table | 450 mm | 400–450 |
| Chair front to table | 400 mm | 400–450 |
| Door swing (1050 mm leaf) | clear of cabinet and chairs | clear |
| Opening to Living | ~1.45 m along the south end of the east wall: confirm | n/a |

**The width, checked.** 357 cabinet + 900 walk + 667 chair + 393 gap + 667 chair + 978 walk = 3,962 mm. With both walks at 900 mm and ≥300 mm between the chairs, any chair up to 75 cm wide fits: (3,962 − 357 − 1,800 − 300) ÷ 2 = 752 mm. If the room is 150 mm narrower than the plan, the gap is still ~243 mm. The Living opening spans the south ~1.45 m of the east wall; you reach it from the 978 mm walk north of the sofa's front edge, so the sofa's east end (761 mm off the wall) never narrows the route.

**Rejected layouts.**
- Two 81 cm chairs (Josmo Biscoff). 357 + 810 + 390 + 810 + 975 = 3,342 mm leaves a 618 mm pinch on the only route to the bench.
- Sofa on the east wall, chairs opposite. The 13 ft width leaves only ~615 mm behind the chairs for the main walk.
- Sofa under the north window facing the gallery. It works, but guests then enter behind the sofa and the window loses its drape stack.
- A 244 cm sofa pushed against the west wall. It frees the east end but puts the art off-centre and leaves no room for the Era 300 and lamp.

| Shoe cabinet + bench | Homz built-in, fluted shutters, slotted or louvred shelves, 20 mm vent gap at top and bottom | ~0.4–0.8L ex-GST if outside the ₹18L (A7 estimate, unverified) | 1830 × 350 mm, full height; bench 900 × 400 | ~11 shelves × 6 pairs is ~60 pairs at best, ~50 in practice (estimate). Large sangat days still need a mat and rack outside the door, if the RWA allows it |
| Art light | 3 Homz ceiling spots (2 over the sofa, 1 at the bench), 5 W, 3000 K, CRI >90, 60°: White Teak Pandora 5 W J-spot, on a trailing-edge dimmable driver and a Sonoff MINI-DIM (§1, §10) | 2,040 each, 6,120 for 3 | 5.7 cm cut-out | **Pandora 15 W, 36°**: rejected, 3–6× the oils limit. **White Teak Frontier Pro magnetic track**: only if the art will move often |
| Speaker | Owned Sonos Era 300 on a ~300 × 300 × 520 mm plinth in the SW corner, on an existing socket (§3) | 0 | n/a | At the east end it would sit in the route to the Living |

**Homz asks.** Draw the shoe cabinet and bench at these sizes. Make the shutters 6 × 305 mm, so one open shutter still leaves a 600 mm walk. Place two spots ~0.6 m out from the south wall, ~1.15 m apart, centred on the sofa. Place one spot ~0.6 m out from the west wall, centred on the bench. All three need a false-ceiling patch, their own switch leg and a trailing-edge dimmable driver behind an access panel. Write CRI ≥90 and the model number into the BOQ, and check a sample tilts ≥25° (§10). No false ceiling: use surface-mount spots on the existing point.

#### Living (19'5" × 11'9", no windows)

<img src="../../../assets/personal/olympus/room-living.svg" width="480"> <img src="https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1772475329856-7c7de908b44e?w=600" width="320">

**Layout.** Media unit on the north (BR-02) wall, centred under the owned 65" TV. KIVIK sofa with chaise against the south (BR-01) wall, chaise at the east end. VIMLE footstool as the table. The play floor is the open corner by the Drawing opening.

| Clearance | Measured | Target |
|---|---|---|
| Drawing-to-Dining walk, media unit to footstool | 1,090 mm | ≥900 |
| Footstool to sofa | 400 mm | 400–450 |
| Eye to screen | ~2.9 m | 65" works from 2.6–2.9 m (§3) |
| Sofa ends to Master and BR-01 doors | clear | clear |

**Why the sofa can't move.** The room has seven doors and openings: Drawing, store, BR-02, closet, Master, BR-01 and the open Dining edge. Both long walls carry two doors each. The longest solid stretch is ~3.9 m of the south wall, between the Master and BR-01 doors, and the 2.8 m sofa takes it. The cost is that the main walk passes in front of the TV.

| Item | Pick | ₹ | Size | Alternatives checked |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Sofa | IKEA KIVIK 3-seat + chaise, Tibbleby beige-grey (§7) | 63,990 | 280 × 95 cm, chaise 163 deep | KIVIK 3-seat without chaise, ₹46,990, 228 × 95 × 83 cm, seat depth 60 cm. Its cover is removable and machine-washable. Frame: particleboard, ply, fibreboard and some solid wood |
| Table | IKEA VIMLE footstool with storage (§7) | **₹29,990** (Festival Oct 2026, was ₹39,990) | ~98 × 73 cm | IKEA STOCKHOLM 2025 Ø90 round coffee table, oak veneer top, solid oak legs, ₹15,990. Rejected: its dark-brown stain breaks the one-wood rule, and a hard edge sits at Kabir's head height |
| Rug | IKEA STOENSE 200 × 300, pale green (§7.2) | 14,990 | 200 × 300 | Polypropylene on synthetic rubber. IKEA says do not wash, so vacuum and spot-clean |
| Media unit | Homz, 2440 × 400 mm, oak-look laminate, fluted doors, 50 mm cable duct to the TV (§3) | ~0.5–1.5L ex-GST if outside the ₹18L (A7 estimate, unverified) | 2440 × 400 | n/a |

**Homz asks.** Find the builder's TV point on the north wall and centre the unit on it. If it misses, §3 says Homz's electrician extends it inside the TV panel. That's one short extension on an existing point, not new wiring.

#### Dining (13'10" × 11'9", E slider to the sit-out)

<img src="../../../assets/personal/olympus/room-dining.svg" width="480"> <img src="https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1636138388621-258a72ecb07e?w=600" width="320">

**Layout.** Table on the room's long axis, east to west. It sits 90 mm off-centre toward the Living, so the slider end keeps 1,100 mm. Three chairs a side. Crockery unit on the solid stretch of the north wall, east of the BR-03 door. It stops ~250 mm short of the east wall, so it stays clear of the slider and leaves room for the drapes to stack (stack them to the south end). Its west drawer, nearest the BR-03 door, holds clean patkas and rumals for anyone entering the Gurudwara (A07e). One print above it, lit by one spot ~0.6 m out.

**Sun on the north wall.** From October to March the sun rises south of east (~25° at the December solstice). Low 7–9 AM rays enter the slider and reach the north wall within ~1.8 m of the glass, at about art height (hand geometry, unverified). So the piece above the crockery unit is a print, ceramic or brass, never an original oil or a textile. Draw the sheer 7–10 AM. That also protects the Fusi: Alankaram's own care note says "avoid direct sunlight".

| Clearance | Measured | Target |
|---|---|---|
| Table long edge to north wall | 1,290 mm, (3,580 − 1,000) / 2 | ≥1,100 to pass a seated guest |
| Table long edge to kitchen edge | 1,290 mm | ≥1,100 |
| Table end to slider | 1,100 mm | ≥900 |
| Table end to Living edge | 915 mm | ≥900 |
| Crockery unit to nearest chair | clear (no overlap in plan) | n/a |
| Crockery unit end to east wall | ~250 mm | clear of the slider |

**Pendant centre:** 2.20 m in from the slider face and 1.79 m from the BR-03 wall. Give Homz these numbers before the ceiling is boarded. The pendant hangs from a ply-backed GI frame member in the Homz false ceiling, or from the builder's existing ceiling hook. Never hang it from bare gypsum board. IKEA lists 3.70 kg, which is likely the packed weight. If no point sits near that spot, Homz's electrician extends the nearest one inside the false ceiling and hard-wires the pendant.

**Pendant choice, by hand calculation (unverified, no photometric file).**

| Option | Output | Lux on the table | Dims? | Verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| White Teak Adonis 30 W, 36°, 1200 mm, CRI >80, ₹9,010 | 30 W × 70 lm/W ≈ 2,100 lm | At 750 mm the beam covers ~1.7 × 0.5 m, so ~1,500–2,500 lux. At 900 mm, ~1,600 | Not stated. A dimmer means a new switch module and a dimmable driver | **Rejected.** About 7× a 150–300 lux dining level (rule of thumb) |
| **IKEA AGUNNARYD 3-lamp, 122 cm, 3 × dimmable ~850 lm E27 LED, ₹9,990 + ~₹1,500** | 3 × ~850 lm ≈ 2,550 lm in deep shades | At ~1 m the three pools overlap over ~3 m², so roughly 400–600 lux at full | Yes, on the Sonoff MINI-DIM (§1). The wall switch always works | **Pick.** Dim to ~40% for dinner. Use plain phase-cut dimmable bulbs: smart bulbs behind a dimmer flicker or fail (corrected 2026-10-02) |
| IKEA SAMBORD 3-lamp, 110 cm, 3 × GU10, ₹4,490 | 3 × ~350 lm GU10 | Brighter pools under each shade | Only with smart GU10 bulbs | Alternative if the ceiling is above ~2.8 m. Its wire adjusts to 150 cm. Polycarbonate shades |

**AGUNNARYD hanging height.** IKEA lists a 0.7 m cord and a 27 cm lamp height. Under a 2.7 m ceiling the shades end ~0.98 m above a 75 cm table. Under 2.6 m, ~0.88 m. Above ~2.8 m they sit over 1.1 m up, which is too high: take SAMBORD. The cord is fixed, so the ceiling height sets the hanging height. IKEA India prints an MRP about twice the selling price on every product, so ₹9,990 is the normal price.

| Item | Pick | ₹ | Size | Alternatives checked |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Table | Alankaram Fusi 8-seat compact, **solid teak** (FUS-CPTK8, §7) | 1,65,000 | 220 × 100 × 75 cm | **Fusi in ash**, ₹1,55,000: its spec line says "Ash/Oak/Beech, Veneer", so rejected (2 Oct). **Phantom Hands Tangāli**, solid teak, 200 × 100 × 73.4, flat-packed (the lift fallback), price by email. **Alankaram Otutu 6-seat ash**, two fluted pedestals, ₹2,15,000, 2000 × 1000 × 750: the fluting motif, at ₹60k more. **IKEA TONSTAD 200 × 85**, oak veneer, solid-wood legs, ₹36,990. **Magari Michigan 6 ft**, ₹93,200, rejected: 183 × 117 cm is too wide, leaving ~700 mm behind the chairs. IKEA TONSTAD extendable 140/196 × 85, ₹39,990 (§7 value) |
| Chairs ×6 (+2) | Alankaram Jeanneret teak dining chair, armless, teak and rattan (§7) | 31,500 each | 48.5 × 57 × 85 cm | Eight fit: three a side take 145.5 cm. Rattan is outside the warranty, so add tie-on pads. **IKEA TONSTAD**, washable cover, ₹8,950, 42 × 48 cm (value). **Magari Acerra**, ₹33,276: upholstered seat and back, fabric outside the warranty. **IKEA LISABO**, ash veneer and solid birch, washable seat cover, ₹8,950, 46 × 51 cm, seat depth 39 cm. An equal alternative: pick on a sit test. It's 4 cm wider, which still fits 3 a side on 220 cm |
| Pendant | IKEA AGUNNARYD pendant with 3 lamps, black, brass-coloured inside, + 3 plain dimmable 2700K E27 LED bulbs, on a Sonoff MINI-DIM | 9,990 + ~1,500 (unverified) | 122 × 22 cm, 27 cm high, 0.7 m cord | IKEA SAMBORD 3-lamp, ₹4,490, GU10. White Teak Adonis 30 W, ₹9,010: rejected, see the table above |
| Crockery unit | Homz, 1050 × 450 mm, closed shutters (no glass, §12). West drawer for patkas and rumals. Only if you take §3's optional Dining ceiling speakers: one vented bay beside an existing socket for the WiiM Amp, with a conduit up the back before boarding | ~0.6–1.0L ex-GST if outside the ₹18L (A7 estimate, unverified) | n/a | IKEA STOCKHOLM 2025 sideboard is 1608 mm wide, too wide for the 1,350 mm wall pier |
| Art light | 1 spot, same as the Drawing (Pandora 5 W J-spot), ~0.6 m out from the north wall | 2,040 | n/a | n/a |

**Look around the corner.** The teak Fusi's page says "master grade solid teak wood"; the ash version's spec line says "Veneer", which is why the teak one is the pick. Its care note says to keep it out of direct sun, and the table end sits 1.1 m from the east slider. Get the species and "solid through" in writing (§7), and ask for detachable legs for the lift. A 220 cm top must fit the service lift diagonally or go up the stair. Measure the car first.

#### Kitchen (13'10" × 13'0", S window)

<img src="../../../assets/personal/olympus/room-kitchen.svg" width="480"> <img src="https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1639751787355-bbc3ed1fd639?w=600" width="320">

**Layout.** The builder's U and platform stay exactly as built (§9). The fridge tall unit sits at the "F" corner and the dry-store base run on the west wall. The sink sits under the south window with a drawer bank and the two-bin pull-out beside it (no dishwasher). The hob and chimney take the east run, with the utility door at its north end. The U leaves 3.0 m of clear floor.

| Clearance | Measured | Target |
|---|---|---|
| West run to east run | 3,000 mm | 1,070–1,200 for one cook |
| With an optional 750 × 1500 loose island, each side | 1,130 mm | ≥1,070 |
| Island to sink run | 1,070 mm | ≥1,070 |
| Work triangle | ~9.7 m | Long, but every point is fixed (§9) |

**Optional island.** The 3.0 m gap is wide, so a loose island on castors adds prep space with no services. Price and product unverified. Skip it if the kitchen proves to have a wall on the dining side, because traffic would then funnel through one door.

**Open or closed to the dining.** The brochure draws no wall between kitchen and dining, while §9's sketch shows a door from the dining. Confirm on site. If it is open, leave it open:

| Option | ₹ (estimate, unverified) | For | Against |
|---|---|---|---|
| Leave it open | 0 | Light from the south window reaches the Dining | Tadka smoke and smell reach the new upholstery and the Gurudwara door |
| Black-framed fluted-glass sliders, 3–4 panels (**Skip**) | ~80k–1.5L for ~120–130 sqft (IndiaMART Hyderabad: fluted glass ~₹95–850/sqft, office partitions ₹150–600/sqft, GST basis not stated) | Closes off smell and keeps light | ~4.2 × 2.7 m of 6–8 mm toughened glass weighs ~170–230 kg before frames (15–20 kg/m²). A gypsum ceiling's GI frame can't carry that, so the track needs slab anchors, which §12 forbids. Panels that wide also need a floor guide, which means drilling the finished tile. Both break the no-civil-work rule |

Cook for four weeks with the §4 chimney on first. If smell still reaches the Dining, any partition needs a structural engineer's OK for slab fixing and the RWA's consent. Treat that as a separate project outside Homz's ₹18L.

| Item | Pick | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Shutters | Greenlam AFX anti-fingerprint matt laminate, warm white, code named in the BOQ | §12 |
| Worktop | Keep the builder's platform. Dark granite, 20 mm, only if there's no platform or it's damaged | §9 |
| Sink and tap | Keep the builder's sink. Only if none: Kohler Strive 30 × 18 in, top-mount in the existing cut-out (undermount needs a new polished cut). Kohler Atom pull-down only if the builder's tap is basic | §9 |
| Hob, chimney, fridge, RO (no dishwasher) | Bosch PNH7O6G20I, Elica FLAT FL 900 MAX, Bosch KCP88E256I, AO Smith Z2 Pro | §4 |

<img src="../../../assets/personal/olympus/room-balconies.svg" width="480"> <img src="https://5.imimg.com/data5/SELLER/Default/2026/2/585654970/KY/BW/JE/9636379/invisible-balcony-grill-500x500.jpg" width="320">

| Space | Size | Faces | Access | Use |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Sit-out | 4'11" × ~8'5" | E, open rail | UPVC slider from the Dining | Morning tea, two folding chairs |
| Master balcony | 13'0" × 4'11" | S, open rail | UPVC slider from the Master | Two folding chairs, one planter on the side wall |
| Service balcony | ~4'11" × 3'8" | E | Off the utility | Check on site: it may hold the VRF outdoor unit or meters. Keep it clear |
| Utility | 4'11" × ~12'10" | E | Door from the kitchen | See the private-rooms section |

**Floor-42 rules.** Fit the full-height grille (§8) before Kabir uses either balcony. Nothing light stays out: fold the table, bring in the chairs and cushions. Nothing climbable sits at the rail. Drill nothing into the façade or the balcony floor slab. The grille fixing is §8's call.

| Item | Pick | ₹ | Alternatives checked |
|---|---|---|---|
| Grille | Full-height vertical invisible grille, SS316 3 mm wire, 50 mm pitch, rail top to soffit, on 4 openings: Master balcony, sit-out, service balcony, utility (§8) | ~35,000–1,20,000 incl. 18% GST for ~250–400 sqft (§8) | §8 owns this line. Demand a mill test certificate: SS202 passed off as SS316 corrodes and snaps. Needs the association's OK |
| Seating | IKEA ASKHOLMEN table + 2 folding chairs, solid acacia, freestanding and foldable, per balcony (§7, 2 Oct). Table 60 × 62 × 73 cm | 10,390 per balcony | **IKEA TÄRNÖ** (the 1 Oct pick, ₹6,890): smaller 55 × 54 table. **IKEA ASKHOLMEN** fold-down wall table: rejected, because it drills into the façade. **IKEA NÄMMARÖ** table doesn't fold: rejected, it stays outside and can step a child up to the rail. **HAVAI teak set** ₹16,990: no published size or warranty |
| Planter | One heavy, low trough on the Master balcony's side wall | ~ | n/a |

Rejected for balconies: decking tiles (IKEA RUNNEN, ₹2,950 per 0.81 m²), because they trap water and dirt over finished anti-skid tile. Umbrellas and lanterns are rejected because the wind lifts them.

#### Site checks that change these plans

| Check | If the answer differs |
|---|---|
| Wall or door between kitchen and dining? | Nothing changes except the optional island, which becomes a poorer idea |
| Dining slider: true width and north end | The crockery unit shortens to ~900 mm if the slider runs closer to the north wall |
| Spare slot on the Drawing and Dining switch plates | Not needed: the Sonoff MINI-DIM sits inside the existing box behind the plate (§1) |
| Each window's face (compass on a phone, at the glass) | The drape and blind choices in §7.2 change. The Drawing's art wall stays south if its window is north |
| Builder TV point position on the BR-02 wall | The media unit shifts or Homz extends the point |
| Ceiling point over the dining table? | If none sits near 2.20 m from the slider, Homz's electrician extends it inside the false ceiling |
| Ceiling height in the Dining | Above ~2.8 m, AGUNNARYD's 0.7 m cord hangs too high: take SAMBORD |
| Does Homz's BOQ include a false ceiling over the sofa wall, bench and dining table? | If not, use surface-mount spots and hang the pendant from the builder's hook |
| Service-balcony contents | If the VRF outdoor unit is there, keep 600 mm clear in front of it |
| Service-lift car and door size | Decides whether the 220 cm table top and the 208 cm KIVIK box ride the lift |

### 7.4 Room designs — private rooms

This section backs the "Room by room — private rooms" brief. It covers the Master, BR-01, BR-02, BR-03 (the Gurudwara), the walk-in closet, the store and the utility. Prices are IKEA India and Amazon.in street prices, GST included, read on 2026-10-01, unless marked "~" (estimate) or "+GST".

#### 7.4.1 How the plans were drawn

| Item | Value |
|---|---|
| Source | Builder brochure p.32, Tower B Flat No. 6 (`assets/personal/my-home-floor-plan.png`) |
| Method | Room sizes from the brochure labels. Door, window and wardrobe positions scaled off the image at about 28 px per foot |
| Scale in the SVGs | 1 ft = 24 px, 40 px margin, north up |
| Files | `assets/personal/olympus/room-master.svg`, `room-br01.svg`, `room-br02.svg`, `room-br03.svg`, `room-closet-store.svg`, `room-utility.svg` |
| Accuracy | Openings are within about ±6 inches. The utility length (~12'4") is scaled, not labelled. **Confirm every opening on site before Homz draws** |

**Which way each room faces.** Read with the entrance on the west, as the brochure's "West facing" label and the owner's balcony check both imply:

| Room | Glazing read from the plan | Old note said |
|---|---|---|
| Master | South slider to the balcony. The plan also shows a small window at the top of the west wall, and one in the master ensuite's west bay | "No west glazing" |
| BR-01 | South window | S |
| BR-02 | North window | N |
| BR-03 | East window, north half of the wall | NE |
| Utility | Open on the east | E |

The balconies face east and south. The Drawing and BR-02 windows face north, and the plan may show a small window in the Master's west wall. **Check the Master's west wall on site.** If a window is there, it takes afternoon sun, and the §7.2 blind plan needs one more blind.

#### 7.4.2 Master bedroom

**Layout.** The main room is 13'0" × 13'0". The entry is a 6'4" × 4'5" dressing nook to the north-east. Its door from the living passage sits in the nook's north wall. The nook has no wall on the room side: the laminate runs straight in (brochure p.32). The builder drew a wardrobe on the nook's east wall. The ensuite (9'0" × 6'0") opens off the north wall, about 6–9 ft from the west corner. The south wall has a 4'11" UPVC slider to the balcony, and the builder's second wardrobe sits west of it.

| Element | Position (ft from NW inside corner) | Why |
|---|---|---|
| King bed, headboard on the west wall | x 0–6.8, y 2.2–8.5 (82 × 75 in outside) | Leaves a 2 ft path along the north wall to the ensuite and nook, and 6'2" from the bed foot to the east wall |
| Bedside, north | Freedom Tree Nara bedside, 46 × 39 cm, solid teak | Room to spare |
| Bedside, south | Homz wall-hung shelf, ~1'0" deep | A table would sit 1'2" from the wardrobe front and block it |
| Wardrobe, south wall | x 0–6.1, 2 ft deep, **sliding shutters** | Builder position. Only 2'7" is clear between the bed and the wardrobe, so hinged leaves can't open past the bedside |
| Wardrobe, nook east wall | 4'5" × 2'0" | Builder position |
| Kabir's gadda, night only | 3 × 6 ft at the bed foot | Rolled away by day. It shares floor with the dastar zone, which is used in the morning |
| Dastar mirror, east wall | IKEA HOVET 78 × 196 cm, south half of the wall | Faces a 1.4 m square of clear floor between the gadda's spot and the slider |

**Why the dastar zone matters.** Tying a dastar takes a full-length mirror, about 1.5 m of standing room, and light on your face from the front. The balcony slider lights you from the side, which shadows one side of the turban.

| Light option | Wiring | Note |
|---|---|---|
| Plug-in floor lamp beside the mirror (IKEA STOCKHOLM 2025, 50 cm fabric shade at face height, ₹9,990, §10) | None, an existing socket | Default. It lights your face evenly from the front-side |
| CRI ≥ 90 downlight ~1 m in front of the mirror | Needs a Homz false ceiling in this room and a short run from the existing ceiling point | Only if the Homz quote already includes a Master ceiling |

**Pipes behind the east wall.** The Master's east wall is the BR-01 ensuite's west wall, and the brochure draws that basin there. HOVET can stand on the floor, tilted and anchored with the fittings in the box (IKEA product page). That needs one small fixing high up instead of four. Homz scans the wall first either way. A mirror shutter on the south wardrobe is no substitute: it leaves only 2'7" of standing room.

**Kabir sleeps here for now,** on a 3 × 6 ft cotton floor gadda at the bed foot (₹995, furnish-picks), rolled away by day. When he moves to BR-02, the gadda goes and nothing else changes. By default (agent-set, owner to confirm), no microphone or camera device goes here while he sleeps in the room. §1 must buy one Echo Dot, for BR-01 only (₹5,499), not three. If you want voice in the Master, change the rule on purpose.

**Floor.** The builder laid laminate. Use felt pads under every leg and a damp mop, never a wet one. A rug at the foot of the bed takes most of the wear.

**Fit for a 6-year-old.** BERGLÄRKA cranks from 52 to 75 cm, so it starts at his height now (~53 cm, EN 1729 size mark 2) and reaches adult height. The top tilts 10°, 20° or 30°, with a U-shaped finger hole so tilting can't pinch. IKEA recommends VIMUND (seat 38–51 cm, braked castors) "for ages from 7", so buy it at 7. Until then he uses a fixed four-leg chair with the foot-rest. An electric frame (ErgoYou E15, 59–124 cm) makes sense at ~10, when he needs ≥62 cm.

| Not | IKEA BOLMEN step (₹599, 25 cm high) | Too tall: his knees would sit above his hips |

Re-check the heights each school year.

**Safety on floor 42.** Fix the sofa-bed, the Homz bench and the wardrobe to the wall. PS 2026 has no mechanism, so it has no pinch point; BERGLÄRKA is hand-cranked, so it has no motor force. Use cordless blinds (§7.2). Fit window restrictors (§8.1). Avoid glass furniture. No Echo, camera or microphone in this room. A plain Bluetooth speaker for bedtime stories is fine.

#### 7.4.5 BR-03 the home Gurudwara

**What the Sikh Rehat Maryada actually says.** Read from the SGPC text of the Rehat Maryada (English, sikhs.org transcription; SGPC's own PDF is image-only). Anything not in this table is Panthic practice, so confirm it with your Granthi.

**Scope.** Art. V sits in Chapter IV, "Joining the congregation", and is written for the gurdwara. A home Prakash follows it by Panthic practice. Art. VIII(a) is the only home-specific article. So every Art. V row below applies at home by practice, not by the SRM's own wording.

| Point | SRM wording | Article |
|---|---|---|
| A home place | "Every Sikh should as far as possible, maintain a separate and exclusive place for the installation of Guru Granth Sahib, in his home" | Ch. VII, Art. VIII(a) |
| Daily opening | "The Guru Granth should be ceremonially opened in the gurduwara every day without fail." | Art. V(b), gurdwara rule, followed at home by practice |
| Closing | Not kept open at night. "Generally, be closed ceremonially after the conclusion of the Rehras." It "should remain open so long as a granthi or attendant can remain in attendance ... Thereafter, it is advisable to close it" | Art. V(b), gurdwara rule, followed at home by practice |
| The place | "should be absolutely clean. An awning should be erected above" | Art. V(c) |
| The seat | "placed on a cot measuring up to its size and overlaid with absolutely clean mattress and sheets", with "cushions/pillows of appropriate kind" | Art. V(c) |
| Cover and whisk | Romalas for covering, kept covered when not being read. "A whisk, too, should be there" | Art. V(c) |
| What is not done | "arti with burning incense and lamps, offering of eatables ... burning of lights, beating of gongs" are "contrary to gurmat". Flowers, incense and scent for perfume are "not barred". For light, "oil or butter-oil lamps, candles, electric lamps" may be lit | Art. V(d) |
| Other books, idols | No book installed at par. No idol worship. No water placed below the seat. No bowing to pictures | Art. V(e), (f) |
| Entering | "take off the shoes and clean oneself up", washing dirty feet. No tobacco or intoxicants on one's person | Art. V(j), (k) |
| Sitting | "Sitting on a cushion, a distinctive seat, a chair, a stool, a cot, etc. or in any distinctive position in the presence of the Guru Granth ... is contrary to gurmat." No one sits bare-headed | Art. V(n), (o) |
| Moving the Saroop | Perform Ardas first. The carrier walks barefoot, though "when the wearing of shoes is a necessity, no superstitions need be entertained" | Art. V(g) |
| Hukam | Take the Hukam early in the morning. If you can't, read or listen later, and "should not give in to a feeling of guilt" | Art. VIII(c) |

**Corrections to the old note.** The old note listed the Manji (cot), the Rumalas and the Chaur as practice. Art. V(c) names all three. The old note called "nothing above the Saroop" an SRM mandate. The SRM asks only for the awning, so "keep the ceiling above clear" is practice, though sensible. The SRM does not say the Saroop may never be left alone in the room. It says to keep it open while someone can attend, then close it. And the old plan to seat sangat on individual gaddis conflicts with Art. V(n): everyone sits alike, on one floor covering.

**Layout.** The room is 13'0" × 11'11". The door sits at the west end of the south wall, off the living. The window is in the north half of the east wall. The builder drew a wardrobe 7'5" down the west wall from the north corner.

| Element | Position | Basis |
|---|---|---|
| Palki Sahib, centred on the north wall | ~3'0" wide × 2'0" deep, 2'6" off the wall | You walk in from the south and face the Guru. The Granthi sits in the 2'6" behind it for Path. Chaur seva is done standing behind or beside the Palki (practice). No direction is required. IndiaMART's 4 ft model is 60 cm deep, so a 3 ft one should be ≤ 2 ft (confirm) |
| Ceiling above the Palki | Plain. No light or speaker directly over it | Practice. The VRF is already installed and stays where it is |
| VRF check (on site) | Find the BR-03 indoor unit and where it blows | If the unit or its air throw falls on the north-wall centre, move the Palki along the north wall, or to the south half of the east wall, below the window. Set the louvres so air doesn't blow on the Palki and rumalas. Never move the VRF: that's refrigerant and electrical work |
| Reading height (Palki spec ask) | A Manji height that suits a reader seated behind it on the floor, or a low reader's platform behind the Palki | Practice. Gurdwaras seat the Granthi on a raised platform behind the Manji (the Thara Sahib photo shows this). Confirm the height with your Granthi before ordering |
| Rumala almirah | The builder wardrobe slot on the west wall, closed Homz shutters | Art. V(c) needs several clean rumalas. **This wall backs the common toilet, WC included, and its lobby basin.** Homz lines the almirah back with laminate as a moisture barrier and leaves a 25 mm air gap. Keep a ThermoPro TP49 hygrometer (₹711) inside. If it reads above 60% RH for a week in monsoon, move the rumalas to a closed box on the north wall |
| Sukhasan | In the Palki, under its rumalas. If the Granthi advises a separate place, a small closed manji on the north wall, in the ~3 ft west of the Palki | The SRM says to close the Saroop ceremonially, not where it rests. A separate Sukhasan place is common practice (unverified as a norm). The old almirah-bay option is dropped: that wall is shared with a WC. The NE corner is out: it sits in the east window's sun |
| Karah Parshad chowki | Small stool beside the Palki, when needed | Practice |
| Clear floor in front of the Palki | 1'4" strip, then the durrie | Room to bow (matha tekna) from the durrie's edge. Chaur seva happens behind or beside the Palki, not here. A 2'6" strip doesn't fit with a 6'1" durrie in an 11'11" room |
| Sangat floor | Plain white cotton durrie ~6 × 9 ft, flush to the south wall, plus a white cotton sheet for Path. Wash the sheet after Karah Parshad | Art. V(n). Seats about 10–12 adults. Ghee and feet stain wool, so wool spot-clean rugs are out |
| Shoes | Removed at the main door, which already happens in the flat | Art. V(j) |
| Hand-wash, head coverings | The common bath and its lobby sit next door. A small basket of clean rumals (patkas) goes by the door | Art. V(j), (o) |
| Kirtan | Sonos Era 100 SL (no microphone) on a small shelf at the south end of the east wall, per §3. Not on the west wall: it is a wet wall | Agent default, not an owner rule: confirm in [[u-setup-instruction]] |
| Light | The existing ceiling point, 2700–3000K. A Homz cove only if Homz builds a ceiling here. Schedule a lamp on a smart plug (§1) | Art. V(d) allows electric light. No dimmer, because that is wiring work |
| East window | Sheer plus a blackout layer (§7.2) | Morning sun is soft, but it fades rumalas over the years |

**What's cut from the old design, and why.**

| Old item | Now | Reason |
|---|---|---|
| Jyot with a glass chimney, a fire blanket in the room, a heat detector instead of a smoke alarm | Cut | Art. V(d) allows electric light. With no flame or incense, the standard §8 alarm works |
| Inline duct fan for incense into the utility shaft | Cut | Ductwork plus electrical work. No incense is planned |
| Resilient-channel acoustic build-up on shared walls (~₹450/sqft) | Cut | Needs plaster and paint work. BR-03 sits at the flat's NE corner, and the plan suggests its north and east walls are outside walls, so it probably shares no wall with a neighbour (unverified) |
| Fluted-oak four-post canopy, lime-plaster backdrop, brass Ik Onkar | Optional | Lime plaster means plastering, which is out of scope. A plain Palki holds the matte, quiet look on its own |
| Larger-sangat build: langar burner bank, 40-pair shoe store, combined sightlines | Open question | Answer the sangat-scale question first (below). None of it is needed for daily Prakash |

| Aizalrug "white cotton" 6 × 8 ft, B0GZ5FYXYT | 5,799 | Listing photo shows jute with two white bands. 2 ratings |
| IKEA TIOKRONA 160 × 230 cm | 4,990 | 100% jute, "Do not wash" |
| Cotton braided rug 8 × 10 ft, B0HCYXKPW8 | 6,399 | Lumpy to sit on for an hour |

**A Palki over a bare Manji, and why.** A Palki carries its own awning, so nothing gets drilled into the ceiling and nothing has to be balanced above the Saroop. It's one object instead of three: a cot, a frame and a cloth. A Manji with a ceiling chandoa costs less, but it needs ceiling fixings above the most sensitive spot in the flat. Ask the Granthi whether a canopy within the Palki's roof meets "an awning above" in your practice. Most Gurdwaras also hang a chandoa (unverified as a rule).

**Open questions.**
- **Sangat scale.** Daily Prakash with the family fits easily. A Sukhmani Sahib or Path with 15–40 people overflows into the living room, with no sightline to the Palki. Decide whether you'll host at that scale. If not, none of the old langar and shoe planning is needed.
- **Saroop.** SGPC publishes no application procedure or lead time (old note, re-checked: nothing on the SGPC site's Rehat Maryada pages). Ask SGPC Amritsar and the Granthi at Gurudwara Sahib Ameerpet, Hyderabad. Start now: it is the longest-lead item for this room.
- **The flat above.** Floor 43 is lived in. Common practice in high-rises accepts this, but ask the Granthi for peace of mind (old note).
- **Bringing the Saroop up 42 floors.** Perform Ardas first. The carrier walks barefoot with the Saroop on the head (Art. V(g)). Book a cleaned, reserved lift car with the RWA, and check its door and ceiling height clear a person standing upright with the Saroop on the head.
- **First Prakash and move-in date.** See A27.5 for timing.

#### 7.4.6 Walk-in closet and store

**Closet (ex-Puja), 4'0" wide × 6'1" deep.** The open side faces east onto the passage to the Master. At 48 inches wide it takes only one storage run. Two facing 22-inch runs would leave a 4-inch aisle.

| Element | Spec |
|---|---|
| Run along the west wall, 22 in deep | Long hang 2'7", double hang 2'0", drawers and shelves 1'6". Open shelves above at the top |
| Aisle | 2'2" (26 in). A drawer at full extension (~20 in) leaves 6 in, so stand beside an open drawer, not in front of it |
| Mirror | Optional NISSEDAL 40 × 150 cm on the north short wall (₹4,990) |
| Door | The ex-Puja is open to the east passage, so Homz supplies the frame and a flush leaf matching the wall colour. It opens outward (no room to swing inside), on concealed hinges with a push latch, with a 10–15 mm floor gap for air. **Hinge it on the north jamb.** Hinged on the south jamb, the open leaf would lie across the Master entry door just south of it. Check the clash on site; if the leaf still fouls the Master door, fit a bi-fold leaf |
| Light | Homz fits a 3000K CRI ≥ 90 downlight or LED profile on the room's existing light point. If it has none, use rechargeable motion-sensor bars. No new wiring |
| Damp | No window and no AC. Keep the floor gap, keep a hygrometer inside (ThermoPro TP49, ₹711), and target 45–55% RH. If it stays above 60% for a week in monsoon, buy a compressor dehumidifier. The Origin silica pod is rated for 25 cu ft, and this room is ~240 cu ft |

**Store (ex-Linen), 5'0" × 3'10".** The door is in the south wall, off the drawing-room end of the living.

| Element | Spec |
|---|---|
| Shelves | 18 in deep on the north wall, 16 in on the east wall. Adjustable pilaster strips, wall-fixed (a child can walk in) |
| Floor bay | Lowest shelf at ≥ 820 mm so a 32-inch suitcase stands under it with wheels. 700 mm takes only a 28-inch case |
| Chemicals | Bulk cleaning stock in a locked box on the top shelf. Keep the in-use bottles in the utility |
| Robot vacuum | Its dock can't live here: a closed door blocks it. Use an open corridor bay (§12) |
| Network gear | Only if the fibre enters near here and a socket already exists (§2). Then add a louvre or undercut, because a router and UPS give off heat |

| Pick | ₹ | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Homz closet and store fit-out (hinges and drawers per §12) | in quote? confirm | Homz BOQ |
| ThermoPro TP49 hygrometer, ×2 | 711 each | Amazon.in B07WCR5Y4B |
| IKEA SKUBB boxes, set of 6, for open shelves | 699 | IKEA India |
| Lockable box for chemicals | ~1,000–2,000 (unverified) | Estimate |

**Cut from the old note:** an inline exhaust fan ducted to a building shaft, with backdraft and fire dampers (ductwork plus electrical work, plus RWA approval). Also cut: a plaster-flush Eclisse or Argenta jib door at ~₹25–70k, since a plaster-flush finish needs skim and paint. A flush laminate leaf from Homz does the job.

#### 7.4.7 Utility

**Layout.** The utility is 4'11" wide and ~12'4" long (scaled). The kitchen opens into it at the north-west. The service balcony lies to the north, and the east side is open. The builder drew the washer on the west wall near the kitchen end and a sink at the south end.

| Element | Spec | Why |
|---|---|---|
| Washer, §4 pick | On the floor at the builder's washer point, 2'0" × 2'0", under a Homz overhang, with a Homz side panel (BWP ply or PVC) or a fitted washer cover on its east face. The utility is open to the east on floor 42: wind-driven rain and morning sun reach the machine | The inner end stays drier. Bosch warns a machine on a base can tip, so no plinth. It leaves 2'9" clear in front |
| Dryer space (Should, §4) | Stacked on the washer with the Bosch kit | The owner asked for a washer-dryer or a stack (9 Sep). Bosch rates the dryer for enclosed rooms, so the bay stays covered and closed on three sides. It runs off a normal socket (§4) |
| Tall cabinet | Homz, 1'6" × 2'0", BWP ply or PVC board, child lock | Brooms, mop, in-use cleaners. Rain reaches here, so no MR ply or MDF |
| Ceiling airer | homWell 6 rods × 6 ft, over the aisle | Needs 7 × 2.5 ft of ceiling. Scan the slab before drilling and never go through it |
| Sink | Builder's, plus the §9 tap if needed | Already there |

**Not here:** the RO (under the kitchen sink, §4), the network gear (§2), the geyser.

**Look around corners.** A front-loader on hard Hyderabad water scales its heater (§4). Floor-42 wind lifts light clothes off an airer, so use pegs. The airer takes light loads and delicates; the stacked dryer takes the rest.

| Pick | ₹ | Evidence |

| Homz tall cabinet | in quote? confirm | Homz BOQ. The brief's thumbnail is IKEA METOD with cleaning interior (₹36,150), shown for the layout only: it's board and would swell here |

### 7.5 Curtains — where to buy in Hyderabad

**Answer:** Buy branded fabric (D'Decor or Sarom) through an authorised dealer that measures, stitches and fits. Bhavani Furnishings in Narsingi is the nearest; Neo Fabrique in Telecom Nagar, Gachibowli gives the second quote. Ask Homz for a ply-backed ceiling pocket at every window, then take Homz's curtain quote only if it beats a dealer on the same fabric code. The value route asks the same dealers for house-brand fabric and their own stitching, on a local aluminium track. It costs about a third as much. Almost all IKEA India panels are 250 cm long, so they fit only a drop of 245 cm or less. The assumed 2.8–2.9 m ceiling rules that out.

#### Top reasons to reject this plan

1. **No route publishes a price.** D'Decor shows no per-metre price online, and neither do Bhavani, Neo Fabrique or Homz. Every made-to-measure figure below is an estimate built from IndiaMART wholesale listings and one branded listing. Treat it as a budget band, not a quote.
2. **No route publishes a warranty.** D'Decor's FAQ covers only online exchanges. Bhavani's "India's Only Full Home Warranty" sits beside "45-Day Installation… for kitchens, wardrobes" on a curtain shop's site, which reads like template copy. Get any warranty in writing on the invoice.
3. **The dealer lists may be stale.** D'Decor's locator dates most Hyderabad entries to 2016–2019. Phone before you drive.

All three gaps apply to every route equally, so they don't separate the routes. An itemised written quote closes them. Get it before you pay anything.

#### Windows and metreage

Openings are traced from the room plans at 1 ft = 24 px, so each width is ±15 cm. **Measure every opening on site.** Track = opening + 15 cm each side. Drape uses 137 cm (54 in) fabric, ~130 cm usable. Each width is cut at the drop plus 25–30 cm. At the 2.8–2.9 m ceiling §3 assumes (**unverified**), that is 3.1–3.2 m. Sheer runs sideways with no seams only if the cloth is at least the drop plus ~20 cm wide. That means 300–320 cm cloth. IndiaMART lists 120 in (305 cm) sheer. With narrower cloth, use seamed 137–140 cm sheer, which needs about as many metres as the drape.

| Room | Opening (traced) | Track | Layers | Drape widths (fullness) | Drape and lining (m each) | Sheer | Best route est. | Value est. | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Drawing | N window ~1.75 m | 2.05 m | Sheer + light-filtering lined drape | 4 (2.5×) | 12.4–12.8 m | 5.4 m | ₹18–41k | ₹6–14k | Low sun. Lining protects the face fabric |
| Dining | E slider ~2.38 m | 2.68 m | Sheer + blackout drape | 5 (2.4×) | 15.5–16 m | 7.0 m | ₹23–52k | ₹8–17k | Hardest sun, 7–10 AM, Mar–Jun. Drape ~10–12 kg (estimate) |
| Master | S slider ~1.49 m (+ a possible ~0.6 m W window) | 1.79 m | Sheer + blackout drape | 3 (2.2×) | 9.3–9.6 m | 4.8 m | ₹14–33k | ₹5–11k | Optional Aqara E1. Add a single sheer if the W window exists |
| BR-01 | S window ~1.19 m | 1.49 m | Sheer + blackout drape | 3 (2.6×) | 9.3–9.6 m | 4.0 m | ₹14–31k | ₹5–10k | Sheer softens call light from the right |
| BR-02 | N window ~1.45 m | 1.75 m | Sheer + blackout drape | 3 (2.2×) | 9.3–9.6 m | 4.7 m | ₹14–32k | ₹5–11k | Blackout for naps. Sage drape fits the palette accent |
| BR-03 | E window ~1.37 m | 1.67 m | Fine sheer + light-filtering drape | 3 (2.3×) | 9.3–9.6 m | 4.5 m | ₹14–32k | ₹5–11k | Sheer only saves ~₹8–20k. Plain white or light beige |
| Living, kitchen, utility | None to outside / not curtained | — | — | — | — | — | — | — | Living has no outside window per plan |
| **Total** | 6 windows | **11.4 m** | | **21 widths** | **65–67 m** | **~31 m** | **~₹1.0–2.2L** | **~₹35–75k** | Plus ~33 stitched panels. Low end at a 2.8 m drop and low rates; high end at 2.9 m and high rates |

IndiaMART rates are wholesale and usually exclude GST (the pages don't say). Retail through a brand dealer runs higher.

| Design Walls | Miyapur | 3rd Floor, MSR Capital, beside Indian Oil petrol pump, near Miyapur Metro Station, 500049 | Sarom | Not checked | The only north-west entry on either brand list. No Madhapur, Kondapur or Kukatpally shop appears on either list, and none was checked |

**Brand presence, verified:** D'Decor (own store + ~17 dealers), Sarom (9 Hyderabad dealers), Sansaar (a D'Decor brand, sold at Studio Orion). **Not found in Hyderabad:** Elementto (a wallpaper house with showrooms in Mumbai and Delhi), Excel and Marvel (no site found). Ask the dealers above if they carry them.

#### Specs that matter on floor 42

| Spec | Choose | Avoid | Why |
|---|---|---|---|
| Face fibre, east and south glass | Polyester or a polyester-rich blend, light colour | Silk, viscose; pure linen or cotton without a lining | Sunlight weakens silk. Dyed cellulose fibres (cotton, linen) photo-fade in sunlight. Polyester shrinks least |
| Fade rating | Ask for lightfastness 5 or more on the Blue Wool scale | "UV protected" with no number | Blue Wool is the test scale for fading. A number lets you compare dealers |
| Blackout lining | **3-pass** foam-coated lining, sewn as a detachable layer | 1-pass "dim-out" sold as blackout | Wikipedia: 1-pass blocks ~50–70% of light; 3-pass blocks ~100% |
| Woven blackout (triple-weave) | Fine where you want one washable layer | Expecting full dark | Woven blackout leaks light (trade claim, **unverified**) |
| Sheer | Polyester voile or linen-look, ~80–120 GSM, weighted hem. Run sideways only in 300–320 cm cloth; otherwise seamed 137–140 cm | 280 cm cloth run sideways | Sideways cloth must be the drop plus ~20 cm for hems and heading. At a 2.8–2.9 m drop, 280 cm cloth finishes 15–30 cm short |
| Heading | Double or triple pinch pleat at 2.0–2.5× fullness | Eyelet (needs a rod, not a ceiling track) | Pinch pleat hangs on standard track gliders. Ripple fold is fine only if the dealer supplies track, tape and gliders as one system |
| Track | Ceiling-fixed aluminium, double channel, ball-bearing runners, a wand on each leading edge | Pull cords, bead chains, PVC tracks | No cord loop for a 6-year-old to reach. Ask the dealer for the track's load rating per bracket in writing |
| Fixing | Into the Homz pocket's ply backer or the slab | Screws into bare gypsum | A lined Dining drape weighs ~10–12 kg (estimate) |
| Washing | Detachable lining; polyester face at 30–40°C | Dry-clean-only fabric on dusty windows | IKEA polyester: machine wash 30–40°C, up to 4% shrink, pre-wash before hemming |
| Flame retardance (optional) | Ask whether the fabric code is inherently flame-retardant (FR) polyester | — | Optional. No rule requires it for homes |
| Motor | One Aqara E1 on the Master blackout, battery-powered, ₹15,220 + hub | Motors on every window; wired Somfy | Somfy India is quote-only (Glydea Ultra, Irismo). The E1 needs a U- or I-rail |

#### How to order

1. **Before Homz draws the ceiling:** ask for a 150 mm clear curtain pocket with a ply backer at all 6 windows, in the ceiling drawing.
2. **After the false ceiling, paint and final clean:** measure each opening, the track length and the ceiling-to-floor drop. Order the drop less 1–1.5 cm so the hem clears the floor.
3. **Swatches:** borrow 3 face fabrics and 2 sheers. Hang them in the Dining and BR-01 windows for one morning.
4. **Quote on one spec sheet from 2 dealers** (Bhavani Narsingi, Neo Fabrique). Each quote must name fabric brand, code and fibre; lining type; heading; fullness 2.0–2.5×; hem weights; track brand and profile; wand count; fitting charge; GST; lead time.
5. **Ask Homz to quote the same sheet.** Take it only if it's cheaper on the same codes.
6. **Pay against delivery,** not in full upfront. Inspect each panel's code label against the quote before fitting.
7. **Fit last,** after the deep clean and before the Prakash and move-in.

- **Silent Gliss.** Its dealer map lists India under Silent Gliss Global Ltd with a UK phone number. No Indian dealer appears. Ask Drapes N More if it imports it.
- **Hettich, Mahavir, Vista tracks.** No India curtain-track product page found. The Curtain Store shows a Vista logo but sits in Gurugram (**unverified**).
- **The Curtain Store, Ellementry, Urban Ladder, Pepperfry, Fabindia, Westside, H&M Home.** None offers verified measure-and-install in Hyderabad. Urban Ladder lists ready-made door curtains. Fabindia's curtain page returned "Not Found"; Pepperfry and H&M blocked checks.
- **IKEA as the main route.** VIDGA and the panels are good. Almost all panels are 250 cm long; the one 300 cm line, MÄVINN, is a striped print. IKEA India shows no sewing service.
- **VIDGA load rating.** The IKEA India VIDGA page gives no load figure (checked 2026-10-01).
- **Distances** in the shop table are map estimates (**unverified**).

### 7.6 Sofa-cum-beds — BR-01 (adult guest) and BR-02 (Kabir)

You asked for a sofa-cum-bed in each room, so this section picks two. BR-01 gets the IKEA HYLTARP 2-seat sofa-bed. Kabir gets the IKEA PS 2026 sofa-bed as his nightly bed, plus an IKEA KOMPISHÄNG fold-out mattress for a friend. On 2 Oct the search widened to ~30 brands at the doubled budget, with Hyderabad makers checked first; IKEA still won both rooms, because it publishes foam density, mattress thickness and open depth, gives 10 years, and can be sat on in Hyderabad. The scored top 5 per room is [7.6.1](#761-top-5-sofa-cum-beds-scored).

#### 7.6.1 Top 5 sofa-cum-beds, scored

*Widened 2026-10-02 to ~30 brands, at the doubled budget (BR-01 envelope ~₹2L, BR-02 ~₹1.5L). IKEA prices re-checked (57 items, unchanged). Hyderabad makers were checked first: Magari sells no sofa-bed, and Alankaram's (Ikili, ₹1.5–2.0L) carries a 1-year warranty and publishes no mattress spec, so it fails criteria C and E below.*

**Dimension anchors (1–10).** **Sl** sleep surface: 10 = one-piece sprung mattress ≥18 cm, 7 = one piece 11–12 cm, 4 = two pieces or a join, 1 = seat cushions or a fold. **Se** daily seat: 10 = seat 43–48 cm and supportive, 5 = usable but low, deep or sloping. **Me** mechanism and frame: 10 = nothing moves, or a steel mechanism with no failure reports. **Co** cover: 10 = machine-washable with a spare sold. **Fi** fit: 10 = fits the room open and closed. **Ch** child safety (BR-02): 10 = nothing moves. **St** style fit. **Wa** warranty and service: 10 = ≥5 years plus local service, 3 = 1 year. **Hy** Hyderabad sit-test: 10 = in stock in a Hyderabad store, 2 = no Hyderabad showroom. **VF** value for money. **Composite** = 0.3·Dur + 0.3·Func + 0.2·VFM + 0.2·Style, with Dur = mean(Me, Wa) and Func = mean(Sl, Se, Fi, and Ch for BR-02).

#### Inversion criteria

A pick that fails two of these is rejected.

| # | Criterion | Fails when |
|---|---|---|
| A | Real sleeping surface | You sleep on seat and back cushions, across a fold or bar, or on foam under 10 cm |
| B | Washable or replaceable cover | The cover is fixed ("non-removable") or no spare is sold |
| C | Durable frame and mechanism | Owners report frame or mechanism failure inside a year, or the warranty is under 3 years |
| D | Fits the room | The open footprint blocks the desk, the wardrobe or the only walkway |
| E | Published spec | Mattress type, thickness or frame material isn't stated |
| F | Child-safe (BR-02 only) | A moving hinge or scissor frame a child can operate or get fingers into |

#### Mechanisms: what fails first

Owner reviews come from IKEA's own review feed, which includes reviews from other IKEA markets. Timings are what owners reported, not lab tests.

| Mechanism | Checked | What fails first | When owners saw it | Verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Pull-out or trundle | HEMNES, FLEKKE, VIHALS, Urban Ladder Bram and Camden | Runners stiffen and the deck creaks. Slats jam or break on the pull-out deck. Two singles side by side leave a seam down the middle for two adults | HEMNES: stiff pull-out from day one and creaking (India review). FLEKKE: slats jam every time (DE 2026), pull-out collapsed at 6 months (US 2026), slats keep breaking (GB 2023) | Fine for children. Middling for two adults |
| Click-clack or futon | NYHAMN, Duroflex Ease, Sleepyhead SofaBed Two, Urban Ladder Peckham, Edo, Salford, Nilkamal Oberon | Plastic slat holders and the ratchet hinge. The mattress creases at the fold | NYHAMN: slats or holders broke at 6 months (US 2024), 8 months (US 2020) and "after a few years" (DE 2026) | Good bed, poor sofa. NYHAMN only, because it has a pocket-spring mattress |
| Fold-out with its own mattress | HYLTARP, LYCKSELE | The mattress keeps a fold crease. The frame won't lock flat. It's heavy to close | LYCKSELE: won't stay flat (GB 2023), crease when stored folded (ES 2023), hard to close alone (AU 2023). HYLTARP: middle bows up (AT 2025) | Best for adult guests. The steel frame itself wasn't the reported failure |
| Sliding "day and night" or fold-forward back | ÄLVDALEN, FRIHETEN, BÅRSLÖV, SALTSJÖBADEN, EKHOLMA | Zigzag springs in the seat soften. You sleep on the seat and back cushions, with a join under your back | ÄLVDALEN: middle soft in 3 weeks (GB 2025), springs failing within a month (KR 2026). SALTSJÖBADEN: "the join between the cushions lands in the middle of your back" (SE 2026). EKHOLMA: "very uncomfortable to sleep on" without a topper (GB 2026) | Reject |
| Modular cushion or folding foam | IKEA KOMPISHÄNG, Wakefit foldable, Sleepyhead Napster | Foam sags and there's no frame under it. It sleeps on the floor | KOMPISHÄNG: too new, 8 reviews, 4.9. Wakefit not verified: its site blocks checks | Reject as a main bed. KOMPISHÄNG is the BR-02 sleepover bed: 13 cm pocket springs, washable cover |
| Daybed, nothing moves | IKEA PS 2026 | The seat is the mattress, so it soils and wears first | Too new: 6 reviews, all 5 stars, Sep 2026 | Best for Kabir |

#### Kabir: nightly bed or sleepovers only?

**Make it his nightly bed, on thin evidence.** IKEA doesn't sell it for that. Its India page calls PS 2026 "a comfy spot for lounging and occasional sleep" and "perfect for daily lounging and occasional guest stays". By contrast, IKEA sells the LYCKSELE MURBO with "Sleep well every night". We overrule IKEA's label on the spec. PS 2026 has one 24 cm mattress of pocket springs under 35 kg/m³ HR foam, on solid-pine slats. That is thicker than MURBO's 10 cm foam, and nothing folds. A UK owner says the mattress can be swapped for a normal single. The likely reason for IKEA's label is wear: the mattress is also the seat, so it gets sat on all day. Re-check it for dips after 12 months.

No paediatric body sets a mattress firmness for a 6-year-old. The AAP's "firm, flat" rule ("it shouldn't indent") covers infants, not school-age children. So this rests on reasoning, and the evidence is thin. What he needs is a flat, one-piece, medium-firm surface with no fold or bar under his hips.

| Option | Sleep surface | Nightly? | Why |
|---|---|---|---|
| IKEA PS 2026 | One piece, 24 cm: pocket springs under HR foam (35 kg/m³), on solid-pine slats | Yes | No fold, no hinge, bed only 41 cm off the floor. A UK owner notes the mattress can be swapped for a normal single later |
| IKEA LYCKSELE MURBO | 10 cm foam (35 and 38 kg/m³), on steel-frame slats, folded when closed | No | Owners call it firm, which suits a child. But the stored fold and the hinge make it a sleepover bed |
| IKEA HEMNES (draft) | Two 80 cm foam singles | Yes, on the top half | A real single on slats, as the old note said. The pull-out is stiff, and the room loses a 160 cm strip when it's open |

**Safety check for BR-02, PS 2026.** No mechanism means no pinch points. The seat edge is upholstered all round and the pine legs sit inside the edge (product photo). The bed is 41 cm high. IKEA's page carries no load-test line, unlike the other IKEA sofa-beds, which state tests "for persons weighing up to 110 kg". The back-cushion covers machine-wash at 40°C. The mattress is "clean with upholstery shampoo" only, so fit a waterproof single protector. IKEA lists the bed as 92 × 204 cm and the mattress as 24 cm thick, but gives no separate mattress size (unverified). Measure the mattress in store. Then buy a deep-pocket protector and fitted sheets that fit 92 × 204 × 24 cm. A standard 90 × 200 may be 4 cm short. IKEA India's waterproof protectors come only in 70 × 160, 80 × 200, 160 × 200 and 180 × 200, so buy these elsewhere. None of the picks cites CertiPUR-US on its India page. IKEA says a packed mattress needs about 72 hours to regain its shape and that any smell fades. Air it for those 72 hours before he sleeps on it.

#### Fit check — BR-01

Room 396 × 396 cm. Measurements run from the west wall (x) and the north wall (y), using the §7.4.3 plan.

- **Where.** HYLTARP goes on the north wall, x 35–217, back to the wall. It takes the draft HEMNES's place and is 27 cm shorter.
- **Closed.** The front edge sits 93 cm off the north wall. The room is unchanged.
- **Open.** The bed's foot reaches y 240. That leaves 95 cm to the wardrobe face at y 335, just over the plan's 3 ft.
- **Sides.** The west edge clears the Homz library front by ~5 cm (BILLY: 7 cm), so the lower shelves are blocked only on guest nights. The east edge is 39 cm from the Steelcase chair.
- **What has to move.** Only the Steelcase chair, pushed under the desk, which opens a ~70 cm path. The ensuite door swings into the ensuite, and the living-room door swing ends at y 85. Neither door touches the bed.
- **NYHAMN instead.** It goes at x 30–230 and opens to about y 150, which leaves the room's middle clear. It sits 26 cm from the chair when closed.

#### Fit check — BR-02

Room 396 × 363 cm. Measurements run from the west wall (x) and the north wall (y), using the §7.4.4 plan.

- **Where.** PS 2026 goes on the west wall, y 91–295, x 0–102. It starts 91 cm south of the north wall, so it isn't under the window and can't be used to climb to it.
- **Open or closed.** The footprint never changes. The play floor (x 122–320) keeps a 20 cm gap. The desk (north wall, x 183–303 with BERGLÄRKA's 120 cm top), the Homz bench (south wall, x 110–290) and the door swing (x 292–384, y 272–363) stay clear. Check the bench's east end against the door swing on site.
- **Sleepovers.** Unfold KOMPISHÄNG on the play floor beside him, at x 105–185, y 91–291. Push the desk chair in. Nothing else moves. By day it stands folded as an 80 × 68 cm pouffe in the play corner. The ₹995 gadda (91 × 183 cm) fits the same spot at x 105–196.
- **LYCKSELE instead.** It goes at x 0–100, y 110–252 and opens east to about x 200, over the west half of the play floor. Roll the rug back and push the desk chair in. An adult opens and closes it.

#### Rejected (7.6)

- **Two HEMNES daybeds (current draft).** Your call is sofa-cum-beds. HEMNES stays the baseline: a reviewer measured its seat at 22 in, high for a daily sofa.
- **FRIHETEN, ÄLVDALEN.** You sleep on seat cushions, and the covers don't come off (A, B). Owners say ÄLVDALEN's springs soften within weeks (C).
- **SALTSJÖBADEN, EKHOLMA.** Cheaper than HYLTARP, with a 161 cm bed that is 21 cm wider. But SALTSJÖBADEN sleeps you on the seat and back cushions across a join, and neither page publishes a mattress or cushion thickness (A, E). Owners of both add a topper. EKHOLMA's sleep surface is unverified, so look at it in store.
- **FLEKKE.** The HEMNES alternative. Board frame, and owners report jammed and broken pull-out slats (C).
- **BÅRSLÖV.** 236 cm won't fit BR-01's north wall beside the BILLY units and chair (D). Rated 3.5 from 207 reviews.
- **Every click-clack except NYHAMN.** A fold sits under your hips, and no mattress spec is published (A, E).
- **Folding-foam sofa-beds (Wakefit, Sleepyhead Napster).** No frame, and the foam is the whole bed (A, C).
- **Godrej Interio.** 1-year warranty, and the specs aren't on the page (C, E).
- **Urban Ladder Bram, held, not rejected.** Solid wood, 36-month warranty, ₹56,999. But the mattress isn't specified (E), and it weighs 110 kg for a floor-42 lift. See it in a store before you consider it.
